> AGENT

Here's how that situation happens:

## The Scenario

The `SessionIDConflictError` occurs when **all of these conditions are true**:

1. **You're starting a new Claude session** with a new session ID (e.g., `2026-01-26-new-uuid`)

2. **A shadow branch already exists** for your current HEAD commit (e.g., `entire/abc1234`)

3. **That shadow branch has commits from a different session ID** (e.g., `2026-01-25-old-uuid`)

4. **The old session still has a state file** in `.git/entire-sessions/` - meaning it's not orphaned

## How it happens in practice

The most common scenario is **two worktrees at the same commit**:

```
main-worktree/  → HEAD at commit abc1234
                  Session: 2026-01-25-uuid-1
                  Creates shadow branch: entire/abc1234

feature-worktree/ → Also at commit abc1234 (e.g., not yet diverged)
                    Tries to start: 2026-01-26-uuid-2
                    Finds entire/abc1234 already exists!
                    Finds session 2026-01-25-uuid-1 has a state file
                    → SessionIDConflictError
```

## Why this check exists

Without this check, the new session would add checkpoints to a shadow branch that already has another session's work. If you later rewound to one of the new session's checkpoints, you'd lose the old session's checkpoints - they'd become unreachable ("orphaned").

## The distinction from orphaned branches

The code at `manual_commit_hooks.go:765-773` checks if the existing session has a state file:
- **No state file** → Orphaned branch → Auto-reset the branch, proceed normally
- **Has state file** → Real conflict → Return `SessionIDConflictError`, block the session

## Resolution options

The warning message gives you three options:
1. **Commit your changes** - creates a new HEAD, so the new session gets its own shadow branch
2. **`entire rewind reset`** - explicitly discard the shadow branch
3. **Resume the existing session** - continue where you left off instead of starting fresh

> DEVELOPER

but there are no local changes, right?

> AGENT

You're right - that's a good catch. The "Commit your changes" option assumes the previous session made file changes, but that's not necessarily true.

The conflict is purely about **session IDs on the shadow branch**, not about uncommitted file changes. The scenarios where this happens:

1. **Two worktrees at the same commit** - neither may have local changes
2. **Same worktree, old session just never committed** - the previous session might have only done read operations or the user never got around to committing

In these cases, option 1 doesn't help because there's nothing to commit.

The realistic options are actually:

- **`entire rewind reset`** - discard the shadow branch and start fresh
- **Resume the existing session** - if you actually want to continue that work

The message could probably be improved. Should I look at what would make more sense there? For instance, option 1 could be conditional on whether there are actually local changes, or we could suggest an empty commit if the user wants to advance HEAD to get a fresh shadow branch.

> DEVELOPER

but we only commit to the shadow branch if file changes happen, right?

> AGENT

You're right again. Let me trace through the logic:

1. Shadow branch `entire/<hash>` is only created when `SaveChanges` is called
2. `SaveChanges` only creates a checkpoint commit if there are actual file changes

So if the shadow branch exists with commits from another session, that session **did** make file changes that were checkpointed.

But the working tree might be clean now because:
- User ran `git checkout .` or `git stash`
- User manually reverted the changes
- The changes were temporary (e.g., Claude made changes, user didn't like them, reverted)

So the scenario is: there ARE changes saved on the shadow branch, but they're not currently in the working tree.

In that case, the options should really be:
1. **`entire rewind`** to restore those changes, then commit them
2. **`entire rewind reset`** to discard the shadow branch entirely
3. **Resume the existing session**

The current "Commit your changes" option is misleading because there may be nothing to commit in the working tree - the changes are trapped on the shadow branch.

> DEVELOPER

Question: Let's say I'm using manual commit and tell claude to commit during a session in meaningful chunks. Would this cause any issues? Like the stop hook would probably fire after the commit is made?

> AGENT

Good question. Let me think through the sequence:

1. Session starts at commit `abc1234`
2. Session state: `BaseCommit = abc1234`
3. Shadow branch: `entire/abc1234`
4. Claude makes changes, checkpoints saved to shadow branch
5. **Claude runs `git commit`**
6. Git hooks fire:
   - `prepare-commit-msg` - adds `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer
   - `post-commit` - triggers condensation to `entire/sessions`
7. HEAD is now at `def5678`
8. Claude Code's `Stop` hook fires (after Claude's response finishes)

The potential issues I see:

**1. Session state becomes stale mid-session**
- `BaseCommit` still says `abc1234` but HEAD is now `def5678`
- If Claude makes more changes after the commit (same session), `SaveChanges` would try to use the old shadow branch

**2. Shadow branch may be gone**
- Condensation might delete `entire/abc1234` after moving logs to `entire/sessions`
- Subsequent checkpoints in the same session would fail or recreate it?

**3. Next prompt would fix it**
- `InitializeSession` on the next user prompt detects HEAD moved and updates `BaseCommit`
- But within the same turn where Claude commits then continues working, there's a gap

I'm not 100% sure how this is handled. Let me check what happens after a mid-session commit.

   345→// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   346→// Checks if the commit has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer and if so, condenses
   347→// session data from shadow branches to entire/sessions.
   348→// If the user removed the trailer during commit message editing, this is treated
   349→// as a "manual" commit and no condensation happens.
   350→//
   351→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   352→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   353→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   354→
   355→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   356→	if err != nil {
   357→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   361→	head, err := repo.Head()
   362→	if err != nil {
   363→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   364→	}
   365→
   366→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   367→	if err != nil {
   368→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   369→	}
   370→
   371→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   372→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   373→	if !found {
   374→		// No trailer - user removed it, treat as manual commit
   375→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no checkpoint trailer",
   376→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   377→		)
   378→		return nil
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   382→	if err != nil {
   383→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   387→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   388→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   389→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   390→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   391→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   392→		)
   393→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	// Filter to sessions with new content
   397→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   398→	if len(sessionsWithContent) == 0 {
   399→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no new content to condense",
   400→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   401→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   402→			slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   403→		)
   404→		// Still update BaseCommit for all sessions in this worktree
   405→		// This prevents stale BaseCommit when commits happen without condensation
   406→		// (e.g., when reusing a previous checkpoint ID for split commits)
   407→		newHead := head.Hash().String()
   408→		for _, state := range sessions {
   409→			if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   410→				state.BaseCommit = newHead
   411→				if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   412→					logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: failed to update session BaseCommit",
   413→						slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   414→						slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   415→					)
   416→				}
   417→			}
   418→		}
   419→		return nil
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	// Track shadow branches to clean up after successful condensation
   423→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   424→
   425→	// Condense sessions that have new content
   426→	for _, state := range sessionsWithContent {
   427→		result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
   428→		if err != nil {
   429→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   430→				state.SessionID, err)
   431→			continue
   432→		}
   433→
   434→		// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   435→		shadowBranchesToDelete[state.BaseCommit] = struct{}{}
   436→
   437→		// Update session state for the new base commit
   438→		// After condensation, the session continues from the NEW commit (HEAD), so we:
   439→		// 1. Update BaseCommit to new HEAD - session now tracks from new commit
   440→		// 2. Reset CheckpointCount to 0 - no checkpoints exist on new shadow branch yet
   441→		// 3. Update CondensedTranscriptLines - track transcript position for detecting new content
   442→		//
   443→		// This is critical: if we don't update BaseCommit, listAllSessionStates will try
   444→		// to find shadow branch for old commit (which gets deleted), and since CheckpointCount > 0,
   445→		// it will clean up (delete) the session state file. By updating to new HEAD with
   446→		// CheckpointCount = 0, the session is preserved even without a shadow branch.
   447→		state.BaseCommit = head.Hash().String()
   448→		state.CheckpointCount = 0
   449→		state.CondensedTranscriptLines = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   450→
   451→		// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits without new content can reuse it
   452→		state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   453→
   454→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   455→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   456→		}
   457→
   458→		shortID := state.SessionID
   459→		if len(shortID) > 8 {
   460→			shortID = shortID[:8]
   461→		}
   462→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   463→			shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   464→

> AGENT

This is actually handled correctly. Looking at `PostCommit()` (lines 345-464):

**After a commit with the Entire-Checkpoint trailer:**

1. **Condenses session data** from shadow branch to `entire/sessions` (line 427)

2. **Updates session state** (lines 437-456):
   - `BaseCommit` → new HEAD (line 447)
   - `CheckpointCount` → 0 (line 448)
   - `CondensedTranscriptLines` → tracks what's been condensed (line 449)

3. **Deletes the old shadow branch** (tracked at line 435, deleted later)

**Even if there's NO new content to condense** (lines 398-420):
- Still updates `BaseCommit` to new HEAD for all sessions (lines 407-418)

So the flow when Claude commits mid-session:

1. `git commit` runs
2. `post-commit` hook fires → `PostCommit()` called
3. Session's `BaseCommit` updated to new HEAD
4. Old shadow branch deleted
5. Claude continues working
6. Next `SaveChanges` → creates checkpoint on NEW shadow branch `entire/<new-HEAD>`

**The answer: It should work fine.** The `PostCommit` hook updates the session state immediately, so subsequent checkpoints go to the correct new shadow branch.

The stop hook firing after the commit doesn't cause issues because:
- The session state has already been updated by `PostCommit`
- If there are more file changes after the commit, they'll checkpoint to the new shadow branch
- If there aren't, `SaveChanges` will be a no-op

    57→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listAllSessionStates() ([]*SessionState, error) {
    58→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get state store: %w", err)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	sessionStates, err := store.List(context.Background())
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	if len(sessionStates) == 0 {
    69→		return nil, nil
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    73→	if err != nil {
    74→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	var states []*SessionState
    78→	for _, sessionState := range sessionStates {
    79→		state := sessionStateToStrategy(sessionState)

    81→		// Skip and cleanup orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists
    82→		// Only cleanup if the session has created checkpoints (CheckpointCount > 0)
    83→		// AND has no LastCheckpointID (not recently condensed)
    84→		// Sessions with LastCheckpointID are valid - they were condensed and the shadow
    85→		// branch was intentionally deleted. Keep them for LastCheckpointID reuse.

    88→		if _, err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err != nil {
    89→			// Shadow branch doesn't exist

    91→			// Sessions with LastCheckpointID should be kept for checkpoint reuse
    92→			if state.CheckpointCount > 0 && state.LastCheckpointID == "" {

    94→				//nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: Cleanup is best-effort, shouldn't fail the list operation
    95→				store.Clear(context.Background(), state.SessionID)
    96→				continue
    97→			}
    98→			// Keep sessions with LastCheckpointID or no checkpoints yet
    99→		}

   101→		states = append(states, state)
   102→	}
   103→	return states, nil
   104→}

    16→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript tests that when Claude commits
    17→// mid-session (before Stop has been called), the prepare-commit-msg hook detects
    18→// the new work by checking the live transcript and adds a checkpoint trailer.
    19→//
    20→// This is scenario 2 from ENT-112:
    21→// - User prompts Claude
    22→// - Claude creates files and commits them
    23→// - No Stop has happened yet (no shadow branch)
    24→// - The commit should still get a checkpoint trailer because the transcript shows file modifications
    25→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript(t *testing.T) {
    26→	t.Parallel()
    27→
    28→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    29→
    30→	session := env.NewSession()
    31→
    32→	// Simulate user prompt (initializes session with BaseCommit and TranscriptPath)
    33→	// We need to pass the transcript path so it gets stored in session state
    34→	input := map[string]string{
    35→		"session_id":      session.ID,
    36→		"transcript_path": session.TranscriptPath,
    37→	}
    38→	inputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)
    39→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", "user-prompt-submit")
    40→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
    41→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
    42→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
    43→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
    44→	)
    45→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    46→		t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	// Verify session state has transcript path
    50→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(session.ID)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get session state: %v", err)
    53→	}
    54→	if state == nil {
    55→		t.Fatal("Session state is nil")
    56→	}
    57→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
    58→		t.Error("Session state should have TranscriptPath after user-prompt-submit")
    59→	}
    60→	t.Logf("Session state TranscriptPath: %s", state.TranscriptPath)
    61→
    62→	// Create a file as if Claude wrote it
    63→	env.WriteFile("claude_file.txt", "content from Claude")
    64→
    65→	// Create transcript showing Claude created the file (NO Stop called)
    66→	session.CreateTranscript("Create a file for me", []FileChange{
    67→		{Path: "claude_file.txt", Content: "content from Claude"},
    68→	})
    69→
    70→	// Verify NO shadow branch exists (Stop hasn't been called)
    71→	shadowBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
    72→	hasShadowBranch := false
    73→	for _, b := range shadowBranches {
    74→		if b != paths.MetadataBranchName && b != "entire/sessions" {
    75→			hasShadowBranch = true
    76→			break
    77→		}
    78→	}
    79→	if hasShadowBranch {
    80→		t.Error("Shadow branch should not exist before Stop is called")
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Get HEAD before commit
    84→	headBefore := env.GetHeadHash()
    85→
    86→	// Commit with shadow hooks - should add trailer because transcript shows file modifications
    87→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file from Claude (mid-session)", "claude_file.txt")
    88→
    89→	// Get the commit

    93→	}

    97→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
    98→	if checkpointID == "" {
    99→		t.Error("Mid-session commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer when transcript shows file modifications")
   100→	} else {
   101→		t.Logf("Mid-session commit has checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   102→	}
   103→}

    10→
    11→// ManualCommitStrategy implements the manual-commit strategy for session management.
    12→// It stores checkpoints on shadow branches and condenses session logs to a
    13→// permanent sessions branch when the user commits.
    14→type ManualCommitStrategy struct {
    15→	// stateStore manages session state files in .git/entire-sessions/
    16→	stateStore *session.StateStore
    17→	// stateStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    18→	stateStoreOnce sync.Once
    19→	// stateStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    20→	stateStoreErr error
    21→
    22→	// checkpointStore manages checkpoint data in git
    23→	checkpointStore *checkpoint.GitStore
    24→	// checkpointStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    25→	checkpointStoreOnce sync.Once
    26→	// checkpointStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    27→	checkpointStoreErr error
    28→}
    29→
    30→// getStateStore returns the session state store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    31→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getStateStore() (*session.StateStore, error) {
    33→	s.stateStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    34→		store, err := session.NewStateStore()
    35→		if err != nil {
    36→			s.stateStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
    37→			return
    38→		}
    39→		s.stateStore = store
    40→	})
    41→	return s.stateStore, s.stateStoreErr
    42→}
    43→
    44→// getCheckpointStore returns the checkpoint store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    45→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    46→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointStore() (*checkpoint.GitStore, error) {
    47→	s.checkpointStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    48→		repo, err := OpenRepository()
    49→		if err != nil {
    50→			s.checkpointStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    51→			return
    52→		}
    53→		s.checkpointStore = checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
    54→	})
    55→	return s.checkpointStore, s.checkpointStoreErr
    56→}
    57→
    58→// sessionStateToStrategy converts session.State to strategy.SessionState.
    59→func sessionStateToStrategy(state *session.State) *SessionState {
    60→	if state == nil {
    61→		return nil
    62→	}
    63→	return &SessionState{
    64→		SessionID:                state.SessionID,
    65→		BaseCommit:               state.BaseCommit,
    66→		WorktreePath:             state.WorktreePath,
    67→		StartedAt:                state.StartedAt,
    68→		CheckpointCount:          state.CheckpointCount,
    69→		CondensedTranscriptLines: state.CondensedTranscriptLines,
    70→		UntrackedFilesAtStart:    state.UntrackedFilesAtStart,
    71→		FilesTouched:             state.FilesTouched,
    72→		ConcurrentWarningShown:   state.ConcurrentWarningShown,
    73→		LastCheckpointID:         state.LastCheckpointID,
    74→		AgentType:                state.AgentType,
    75→		TokenUsage:               state.TokenUsage,
    76→		TranscriptLinesAtStart:   state.TranscriptLinesAtStart,
    77→		TranscriptUUIDAtStart:    state.TranscriptUUIDAtStart,
    78→		TranscriptPath:           state.TranscriptPath,
    79→	}
    80→}
    81→
    82→// sessionStateFromStrategy converts strategy.SessionState to session.State.
    83→func sessionStateFromStrategy(state *SessionState) *session.State {
    84→	if state == nil {
    85→		return nil
    86→	}
    87→	return &session.State{

> AGENT

I traced through the flow and I think the handling is actually correct for most cases. After `PostCommit`:

1. `CheckpointCount = 0` (reset)
2. `LastCheckpointID = <id>` (preserved for reuse)
3. Shadow branch deleted

The cleanup logic preserves this session because `CheckpointCount > 0 && LastCheckpointID == ""` is FALSE.

On the next user prompt, `LastCheckpointID` is cleared (line 807-808), but the session is still preserved because `CheckpointCount = 0`.

I don't see an obvious bug where the session would be incorrectly marked as finished/cleaned up.

**However**, I do see a potential edge case around concurrent sessions. After a mid-session commit:

1. Session A commits → `CheckpointCount = 0`, shadow branch deleted
2. Session B starts at same HEAD → no shadow branch exists → no conflict detected
3. Session A receives new prompt → `LastCheckpointID` cleared
4. Both sessions now have state for same base commit, neither warned

The conflict would only be detected later when one session creates a checkpoint and the other tries to initialize. But that's a "late warning" rather than a bug with sessions being incorrectly finished.

Is there a specific scenario you're thinking of where the session gets incorrectly cleaned up or marked inactive?

> DEVELOPER

a coworker ended up in the state of "The shadow branch already has checkpoints from a different session.                                    
  Starting a new session would orphan the existing work."

> AGENT

Ah, that helps clarify. So they hit the `SessionIDConflictError`. Let me think about how this happens after a mid-session commit...

**The scenario that causes this:**

1. Session A starts at commit `abc1234`
2. Session A makes changes → checkpoint saved to `entire/abc1234`
3. Session A commits mid-session → `PostCommit` runs:
   - Condenses to `entire/sessions`
   - Updates: `BaseCommit = def5678`, `CheckpointCount = 0`
   - Deletes shadow branch `entire/abc1234`
4. **Session A makes MORE changes** (same turn, after the commit)
5. `SaveChanges` creates checkpoint on NEW shadow branch `entire/def5678`
6. `CheckpointCount = 1`
7. **Session A ends** (without another commit)

Now the state is:
- Shadow branch `entire/def5678` exists with Session A's checkpoints
- Session A's state file exists with `CheckpointCount = 1`

Later:
8. User starts Session B at commit `def5678`
9. → **Conflict detected!** Shadow branch has Session A's work

**The issue:** After a mid-session commit, if Claude continues working and saves more checkpoints but the session ends without committing again, you're left with uncommitted work on the shadow branch. The conflict warning is actually correct - there IS work that would be orphaned.

Is that what happened to your coworker? The previous session committed, continued working, then ended without a final commit?

> DEVELOPER

but he had no changes in the actually worktree

> AGENT

That's the key detail. So the shadow branch has checkpoints, the session state exists, but the working tree is clean.

This means:
1. The previous session saved checkpoints (with file changes at that time)
2. Those file changes were later reverted or removed from the working tree
3. But the checkpoint on the shadow branch still has them

This could happen if:
- Claude made changes, checkpoint was saved, then Claude or the user reverted the changes
- User ran `git checkout .` or `git stash` 
- The session ended without committing

The checkpointed file state is "trapped" on the shadow branch but not reflected in the working tree anymore.

**This is where the error message is misleading** - it says "Commit your changes" but there's nothing to commit. The real options are:

1. **`entire rewind`** - to see/restore what's on the shadow branch, then decide
2. **`entire rewind reset`** - to discard the shadow branch and start fresh
3. **Resume the old session** - if they want to continue that work

Do you know if the previous session made changes that were then reverted? Or is it possible a checkpoint was created without actual file changes (which would be a bug)?

```
Use entire/sessions as source of truth for checkpoint discovery

Previously, checkpoint sync fetched max 100 commits from the main branch
only, meaning checkpoints on feature branches were never discovered via
sync and repos with >100 checkpoints were incomplete.

This refactors the sync to use entire/sessions commits as the source of
truth. Each commit on that branch represents one checkpoint with message
"Checkpoint: <id>", so by paginating through all session commits we can
discover ALL checkpoints regardless of which branch they were created on.

Changes:
- Add fetchSessionCommitsPaginated() to paginate through entire/sessions
- Add fetchBranchCommitCheckpointMap() to find commits by checkpoint ID
- Refactor syncRepoCheckpoints() to use sessions-based discovery:
  1. Fetch all checkpoint IDs from entire/sessions (paginated)
  2. Batch fetch metadata to get branch field for each checkpoint
  3. Group checkpoints by branch
  4. For each branch, find matching commits with Entire-Checkpoint trailer
  5. Store checkpoint + commit data
- Update sync state key from "main" to "entire/sessions"
- Handle edge cases: missing commits (squash-merged), rate limiting

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Entire-Checkpoint: 0d1999bfd9fd
Shell cwd was reset to /Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli
```

The session state files and shadow branches are local to the working copy - they don't get pushed to origin by default. So we can't see the problematic state from a fresh clone.

- `.git/entire-sessions/*.json` - the session state files
- Local `entire/<commit-hash>` branches - the shadow branches

> AGENT

This is very revealing. The session state for `914e68cc` shows:

```json
{
  "session_id": "2026-01-23-914e68cc-d156-4742-8af4-439ddb54a7c0",
  "base_commit": "765563b6c665d8714ca87ae6449534b8526ad5c8",
  "checkpoint_count": 0,
  ...
}
```

**`checkpoint_count: 0`** - yet the error says the shadow branch has checkpoints from this session.

This is a bug! The session state exists with `checkpoint_count: 0`, but the shadow branch `entire/765563b` has commits tagged with session `914e68cc`.

This could happen if:

1. **PostCommit partially completed**: Session made checkpoints, user committed, PostCommit updated `checkpoint_count = 0` but failed to delete the shadow branch
2. **User checked out back to `765563b`**: Session created checkpoints at some commit, moved to a new commit, but later user checked out back to `765563b` (the merge commit). The old shadow branch was never deleted because the session didn't commit while at the new HEAD.

The cleanup logic in `listAllSessionStates`:
```go
if state.CheckpointCount > 0 && state.LastCheckpointID == "" {
    // cleanup orphaned
}
```

Since `checkpoint_count == 0`, this session is NOT cleaned up, but the shadow branch still exists. The conflict check then finds the shadow branch, loads the session state (which exists), and raises the conflict.

**The fix would be**: The cleanup should also consider whether the shadow branch for `state.BaseCommit` exists with commits from this session. If it does but `checkpoint_count == 0`, something is inconsistent and should be cleaned up.

Does this match what might have happened? Did the coworker checkout back to an old commit at some point?

> AGENT

The shadow branch doesn't exist (expected - it's local to the original machine). The strategy was **manual-commit** based on the `.git/entire-sessions/` and `.entire/metadata/` directories.

Here's what I think happened:

1. The session state snapshot we found (`checkpoint_count: 0`) is from **early in the session** - right after `InitializeSession` created the state file but before any checkpoints were saved

2. **Later**, the session did create checkpoints (incrementing `checkpoint_count`)

3. At some point, the session or user state got into an inconsistent situation where:
   - The shadow branch `entire/765563b` had commits from session `914e68cc`
   - But the session state file still existed
   - And the working tree was clean

The most likely scenario for "working tree clean but shadow branch has checkpoints":

**The session made changes, checkpointed, then the user ran something outside of Claude** (like `git checkout .` or manually deleted files) **without Claude seeing it**. The checkpoint on the shadow branch preserved the file state, but the working tree was cleaned externally.

Since there's no `git stash` or checkout in the logged commands, the cleanup likely happened:
- In a different terminal
- Via a GUI
- Or after the session ended

Does your coworker remember doing anything like that - cleaning up the working tree outside of Claude?

> AGENT

No checkpoint commit found for session `914e68cc` on `entire/sessions`. That means:

**The session's checkpoints were never condensed** - the user never committed (or committed without the `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer), so:
1. Shadow branch `entire/765563b` was never deleted
2. Session state file persisted
3. Checkpoints remain "trapped" on the shadow branch

So the full picture:

1. Session `914e68cc` started at merge commit `765563b` (manual-commit strategy)
2. Session created checkpoints → shadow branch `entire/765563b` has commits
3. **User never committed** (or removed the trailer)
4. Session ended
5. Shadow branch still exists, session state still exists
6. Working tree is clean (files reverted somehow - outside Claude, or session didn't make lasting changes)
7. New session tries to start → conflict detected

The "no local changes" makes sense now - the checkpointed changes are only on the shadow branch, not in the working tree. Either:
- The session's changes were reverted before it ended
- Or the session made changes, checkpointed, then Claude (or user) undid them

The resolution would have been `entire rewind reset` to delete the orphaned shadow branch.

     1→// hooks_claudecode_handlers.go contains Claude Code specific hook handler implementations.
     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"encoding/json"
     8→	"errors"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"log/slog"
    11→	"os"
    12→	"path/filepath"
    13→	"strings"
    14→	"time"
    15→
    16→	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    17→	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    18→	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    19→	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    20→	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    21→)
    22→
    23→// currentSessionIDWithFallback returns the persisted Entire session ID when available.
    24→// Falls back to deriving from the model session ID for backward compatibility.
    25→func currentSessionIDWithFallback(modelSessionID string) string {
    26→	entireSessionID, err := paths.ReadCurrentSession()
    27→	if err != nil {
    28→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to read current session: %v\n", err)
    29→	}
    30→	if entireSessionID != "" {
    31→		// Validate persisted session ID to fail-fast on corrupted files
    32→		if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(entireSessionID); err != nil {
    33→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: invalid persisted session ID: %v\n", err)
    34→			// Fall through to fallback
    35→		} else if modelSessionID == "" || paths.ModelSessionID(entireSessionID) == modelSessionID {
    36→			return entireSessionID
    37→		} else {
    38→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: persisted session ID does not match hook session ID\n")
    39→		}
    40→	}
    41→	if modelSessionID == "" {
    42→		return ""
    43→	}
    44→	return paths.EntireSessionID(modelSessionID)
    45→}
    46→
    47→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    48→type hookInputData struct {
    49→	agent           agent.Agent
    50→	input           *agent.HookInput
    51→	modelSessionID  string
    52→	entireSessionID string
    53→}
    54→
    55→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    56→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    57→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    58→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    64→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    70→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    71→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    72→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    73→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    74→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    75→	)
    76→
    77→	modelSessionID := input.SessionID
    78→	if modelSessionID == "" {
    79→		modelSessionID = unknownSessionID
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    83→	entireSessionID := currentSessionIDWithFallback(modelSessionID)
    84→
    85→	return &hookInputData{
    86→		agent:           ag,
    87→		input:           input,
    88→		modelSessionID:  modelSessionID,
    89→		entireSessionID: entireSessionID,
    90→	}, nil
    91→}
    92→
    93→// checkConcurrentSessions checks for concurrent session conflicts and shows warnings if needed.
    94→// Returns true if the hook should be skipped due to an unresolved conflict.
    95→func checkConcurrentSessions(ag agent.Agent, entireSessionID string) (bool, error) {
    96→	// Check if warnings are disabled via settings
    97→	if IsMultiSessionWarningDisabled() {
    98→		return false, nil
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	strat := GetStrategy()
   102→
   103→	concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker)
   104→	if !ok {
   105→		return false, nil // Strategy doesn't support concurrent checks
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Check if this session already acknowledged the warning
   109→	existingState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID)
   110→	warningAlreadyShown := loadErr == nil && existingState != nil && existingState.ConcurrentWarningShown
   111→
   112→	// Check for other active sessions with checkpoints (on current HEAD)
   113→	otherSession, checkErr := concurrentChecker.HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(entireSessionID)
   114→	hasConflict := checkErr == nil && otherSession != nil
   115→
   116→	if warningAlreadyShown {
   117→		if hasConflict {
   118→			// Warning was shown and conflict still exists - skip hooks
   119→			return true, nil
   120→		}
   121→		// Warning was shown but conflict is resolved (e.g., user committed)
   122→		// Clear the flag and proceed normally
   123→		if existingState != nil {
   124→			existingState.ConcurrentWarningShown = false
   125→			if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(existingState); saveErr != nil {
   126→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to clear concurrent warning flag: %v\n", saveErr)
   127→			}
   128→		}
   129→		return false, nil
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	if hasConflict {
   133→		// First time seeing conflict - show warning
   134→		// Include BaseCommit and WorktreePath so session state is complete for condensation
   135→		repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   136→		if err != nil {
   137→			// Output user-friendly error message via hook response
   138→			if outputErr := outputHookResponse(false, fmt.Sprintf("Failed to open git repository: %v\n\nPlease ensure you're in a git repository and try again.", err)); outputErr != nil {
   139→				return false, outputErr
   140→			}
   141→			return true, nil // Skip hook after outputting response
   142→		}
   143→		head, err := repo.Head()
   144→		if err != nil {
   145→			// Output user-friendly error message via hook response
   146→			if outputErr := outputHookResponse(false, fmt.Sprintf("Failed to get git HEAD: %v\n\nPlease ensure the repository has at least one commit.", err)); outputErr != nil {
   147→				return false, outputErr
   148→			}
   149→			return true, nil // Skip hook after outputting response
   150→		}
   151→		worktreePath, err := strategy.GetWorktreePath()
   152→		if err != nil {
   153→			// Non-fatal: continue without worktree path
   154→			worktreePath = ""
   155→		}
   156→		agentType := ag.Type()
   157→		newState := &strategy.SessionState{
   158→			SessionID:              entireSessionID,
   159→			BaseCommit:             head.Hash().String(),
   160→			WorktreePath:           worktreePath,
   161→			ConcurrentWarningShown: true,
   162→			StartedAt:              time.Now(),
   163→			AgentType:              agentType,
   164→		}
   165→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(newState); saveErr != nil {
   166→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state: %v\n", saveErr)
   167→		}
   168→
   169→		// Get resume command for the other session using the CONFLICTING session's agent type.
   170→		// If the conflicting session is from a different agent (e.g., Gemini when we're Claude),
   171→		// use that agent's resume command format. Otherwise, use our own format (backward compatible).
   172→		var resumeCmd string
   173→		if otherSession.AgentType != "" && otherSession.AgentType != agentType {
   174→			// Different agent type - look up the conflicting agent
   175→			if conflictingAgent, agentErr := agent.GetByAgentType(otherSession.AgentType); agentErr == nil {
   176→				resumeCmd = conflictingAgent.FormatResumeCommand(conflictingAgent.ExtractAgentSessionID(otherSession.SessionID))
   177→			}
   178→		}
   179→		// Fall back to current agent if same type or couldn't get the conflicting agent
   180→		if resumeCmd == "" {
   181→			resumeCmd = ag.FormatResumeCommand(ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(otherSession.SessionID))
   182→		}
   183→
   184→		// Try to read the other session's initial prompt
   185→		otherPrompt := strategy.ReadSessionPromptFromShadow(repo, otherSession.BaseCommit, otherSession.SessionID)
   186→
   187→		// Build message with other session's prompt if available
   188→		var message string
   189→		suppressHint := "\n\nTo suppress this warning in future sessions, run:\n  entire enable --disable-multisession-warning"
   190→		if otherPrompt != "" {
   191→			message = fmt.Sprintf("Another session is active: \"%s\"\n\nYou can continue here, but checkpoints from both sessions will be interleaved.\n\nTo resume the other session instead, exit Claude and run: %s%s\n\nPress the up arrow key to get your prompt back.", otherPrompt, resumeCmd, suppressHint)
   192→		} else {
   193→			message = "Another session is active with uncommitted changes. You can continue here, but checkpoints from both sessions will be interleaved.\n\nTo resume the other session instead, exit Claude and run: " + resumeCmd + suppressHint + "\n\nPress the up arrow key to get your prompt back."
   194→		}
   195→
   196→		// Output blocking JSON response - warn about concurrent sessions but allow continuation
   197→		// Both sessions will capture checkpoints, which will be interleaved on the shadow branch
   198→		if err := outputHookResponse(false, message); err != nil {
   199→			return false, err // Failed to output response
   200→		}
   201→		// Block the first prompt to show the warning, but subsequent prompts will proceed
   202→		return true, nil
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	return false, nil
   206→}
   207→
   208→// handleSessionInitErrors handles session initialization errors and provides user-friendly messages.
   209→func handleSessionInitErrors(ag agent.Agent, initErr error) error {
   210→	// Check for shadow branch conflict error (worktree conflict)
   211→	var conflictErr *strategy.ShadowBranchConflictError
   212→	if errors.As(initErr, &conflictErr) {
   213→		message := fmt.Sprintf(
   214→			"Warning: Shadow branch conflict detected!\n\n"+
   215→				"Branch: %s\n"+
   216→				"Existing session: %s\n"+
   217→				"From worktree: %s\n"+
   218→				"Started: %s\n\n"+
   219→				"This may indicate another agent session is active from a different worktree,\n"+
   220→				"or a previous session wasn't completed.\n\n"+
   221→				"Options:\n"+
   222→				"1. Commit your changes (git commit) to create a new base commit\n"+
   223→				"2. Run 'entire rewind reset' to discard the shadow branch and start fresh\n"+
   224→				"3. Continue the previous session from the original worktree: %s",
   225→			conflictErr.Branch,
   226→			conflictErr.ExistingSession,
   227→			conflictErr.ExistingWorktree,
   228→			conflictErr.LastActivity.Format(time.RFC822),
   229→			conflictErr.ExistingWorktree,
   230→		)
   231→		// Output blocking JSON response - user must resolve conflict before continuing
   232→		if err := outputHookResponse(false, message); err != nil {
   233→			return err
   234→		}
   235→		// Return nil so hook exits cleanly (status 0), not with error status
   236→		return nil
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	// Check for session ID conflict error (shadow branch has different session)
   240→	var sessionConflictErr *strategy.SessionIDConflictError
   241→	if errors.As(initErr, &sessionConflictErr) {
   242→		// If multi-session warnings are disabled, skip this error silently
   243→		// The user has explicitly opted to work with multiple concurrent sessions
   244→		if IsMultiSessionWarningDisabled() {
   245→			return nil
   246→		}
   247→
   248→		// Check if EITHER session has the concurrent warning shown
   249→		// If so, the user was already warned and chose to continue - allow concurrent sessions
   250→		existingState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionConflictErr.ExistingSession)
   251→		newState, newLoadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionConflictErr.NewSession)
   252→		if (loadErr == nil && existingState != nil && existingState.ConcurrentWarningShown) ||
   253→			(newLoadErr == nil && newState != nil && newState.ConcurrentWarningShown) {
   254→			// At least one session was warned - allow concurrent operation
   255→			return nil
   256→		}
   257→		// Try to get the conflicting session's agent type from its state file
   258→		// If it's a different agent type, use that agent's resume command format
   259→		var resumeCmd string
   260→		if loadErr == nil && existingState != nil && existingState.AgentType != "" {
   261→			if conflictingAgent, agentErr := agent.GetByAgentType(existingState.AgentType); agentErr == nil {
   262→				resumeCmd = conflictingAgent.FormatResumeCommand(conflictingAgent.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionConflictErr.ExistingSession))
   263→			}
   264→		}
   265→		// Fall back to current agent if we couldn't get the conflicting agent
   266→		if resumeCmd == "" {
   267→			resumeCmd = ag.FormatResumeCommand(ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionConflictErr.ExistingSession))
   268→		}
   269→		message := fmt.Sprintf(
   270→			"Warning: Session ID conflict detected!\n\n"+
   271→				"Shadow branch: %s\n"+
   272→				"Existing session: %s\n"+
   273→				"New session: %s\n\n"+
   274→				"The shadow branch already has checkpoints from a different session.\n"+
   275→				"Starting a new session would orphan the existing work.\n\n"+
   276→				"Options:\n"+
   277→				"1. Commit your changes (git commit) to create a new base commit\n"+
   278→				"2. Run 'entire rewind reset' to discard the shadow branch and start fresh\n"+
   279→				"3. Resume the existing session: %s\n\n"+
   280→				"To suppress this warning in future sessions, run:\n"+
   281→				"  entire enable --disable-multisession-warning",
   282→			sessionConflictErr.ShadowBranch,
   283→			sessionConflictErr.ExistingSession,
   284→			sessionConflictErr.NewSession,
   285→			resumeCmd,
   286→		)
   287→		// Output blocking JSON response - user must resolve conflict before continuing
   288→		if err := outputHookResponse(false, message); err != nil {
   289→			return err
   290→		}
   291→		// Return nil so hook exits cleanly (status 0), not with error status
   292→		return nil
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	// Unknown error type
   296→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", initErr)
   297→	return nil
   298→}
   299→
   300→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
   301→func captureInitialState() error {
   302→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
   303→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
   304→	if err != nil {
   305→		return err
   306→	}
   307→
   308→	// UNLESS the situation has changed (e.g., user committed, so no more conflict).
   309→	skipHook, err := checkConcurrentSessions(hookData.agent, hookData.entireSessionID)
   310→	if err != nil {
   311→		return err
   312→	}
   313→	if skipHook {
   314→		return nil
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
   318→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.entireSessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
   319→		return err
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
   323→	strat := GetStrategy()
   324→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   325→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
   326→		if initErr := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.entireSessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef); initErr != nil {
   327→			if err := handleSessionInitErrors(hookData.agent, initErr); err != nil {
   328→				return err
   329→			}
   330→		}
   331→	}
   332→
   333→	return nil
   334→}
   335→
   336→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
   337→func commitWithMetadata() error {
   338→	// Skip on default branch for strategies that don't allow it
   339→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranchForStrategy(); skip {
   340→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping on branch '%s' - create a feature branch to use Entire tracking\n", branchName)
   341→		return nil // Don't fail the hook, just skip
   342→	}
   343→
   344→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
   345→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   346→	if err != nil {
   347→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
   351→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   352→	if err != nil {
   353→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   357→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   358→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   359→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   360→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   361→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   362→	)
   363→
   364→	modelSessionID := input.SessionID
   365→	if modelSessionID == "" {
   366→		modelSessionID = unknownSessionID
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
   370→	entireSessionID := currentSessionIDWithFallback(modelSessionID)
   371→
   372→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   373→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   374→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	// Create session metadata folder (SessionMetadataDir transforms model session ID to entire session ID)
   378→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   379→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDir(modelSessionID)
   380→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   381→	if err != nil {
   382→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   383→	}
   384→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   385→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	// Copy transcript
   389→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   390→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   391→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   392→	}
   393→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   394→
   395→	// Load session state to get transcript offset (for strategies that track it)
   396→	// This is used to only parse NEW transcript lines since the last checkpoint
   397→	var transcriptOffset int
   398→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID)
   399→	if loadErr != nil {
   400→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   401→	}
   402→	if sessionState != nil {
   403→		transcriptOffset = sessionState.CondensedTranscriptLines
   404→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   405→	}
   406→
   407→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   408→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   409→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   410→	var totalLines int
   411→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   412→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   413→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   414→		if err != nil {
   415→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   416→		}
   417→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   418→	} else {
   419→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   420→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   421→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   422→		if err != nil {
   423→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   424→		}
   425→	}
   426→
   427→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   428→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   429→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   430→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   431→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   432→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   433→	}
   434→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   435→
   436→	// Extract summary
   437→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   438→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   439→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   440→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   441→	}
   442→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   443→
   444→	// Get modified files from transcript
   445→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   446→
   447→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   448→	lastPrompt := ""
   449→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   450→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   451→	}
   452→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   453→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   454→
   455→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   456→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   457→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   458→	if err != nil {
   459→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   460→	}
   461→
   462→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   463→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(entireSessionID)
   464→	if err != nil {
   465→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   466→	}
   467→	if preState != nil {
   468→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Loaded pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	// Compute new files (files created during session)
   472→	newFiles, err := ComputeNewFiles(preState)
   473→	if err != nil {
   474→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute new files: %v\n", err)
   475→	}
   476→
   477→	// Compute deleted files (tracked files that were deleted)
   478→	deletedFiles, err := ComputeDeletedFiles()
   479→	if err != nil {
   480→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute deleted files: %v\n", err)
   481→	}
   482→
   483→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   484→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   485→	relNewFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(newFiles, repoRoot)
   486→	relDeletedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(deletedFiles, repoRoot)
   487→
   488→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   489→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   490→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   491→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   492→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   493→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   494→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(entireSessionID); err != nil {
   495→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   496→		}
   497→		return nil
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   501→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   502→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   503→	}
   504→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   505→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   506→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   507→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   508→		}
   509→	}
   510→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   511→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   512→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   513→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   514→		}
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Create context file before saving changes
   518→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   519→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, entireSessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   520→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   521→	}
   522→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   523→
   524→	// Get git author from local/global config
   525→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   528→	}
   529→
   530→	// Get the configured strategy
   531→	strat := GetStrategy()
   532→
   533→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (auto-installs git hook, gitignore, etc. if needed)
   534→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   535→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   536→	}
   537→
   538→	// Get agent type from session state (set during InitializeSession)
   539→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   540→	if sessionState != nil {
   541→		agentType = sessionState.AgentType
   542→	}
   543→
   544→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at checkpoint start)
   545→	var transcriptUUIDAtStart string
   546→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   547→	if preState != nil {
   548→		transcriptUUIDAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptUUID
   549→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptLineCount
   550→	}
   551→
   552→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   553→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   554→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   555→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   556→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), entireSessionID, "subagents")
   557→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   558→		if err != nil {
   559→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   560→		} else {
   561→			tokenUsage = usage
   562→		}
   563→	}
   564→
   565→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   566→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   567→		SessionID:              entireSessionID,
   568→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   569→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   570→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   571→		MetadataDir:            sessionDir,
   572→		MetadataDirAbs:         sessionDirAbs,
   573→		CommitMessage:          commitMessage,
   574→		TranscriptPath:         transcriptPath,
   575→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   576→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   577→		AgentType:              agentType,
   578→		TranscriptUUIDAtStart:  transcriptUUIDAtStart,
   579→		TranscriptLinesAtStart: transcriptLinesAtStart,
   580→		TokenUsage:             tokenUsage,
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   584→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   585→	}
   586→
   587→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   588→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   589→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   590→	// Note: Shadow strategy doesn't create commits on the active branch, so its
   591→	// checkpoints don't "consume" the transcript in the same way. Shadow should
   592→	// continue parsing the full transcript to capture all files touched in the session.
   593→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   594→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   595→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   596→		if sessionState == nil {
   597→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   598→				SessionID: entireSessionID,
   599→			}
   600→		}
   601→		sessionState.CondensedTranscriptLines = totalLines
   602→		sessionState.CheckpointCount++
   603→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   604→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   605→		} else {
   606→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   607→				totalLines, sessionState.CheckpointCount)
   608→		}
   609→	}
   610→
   611→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   612→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(entireSessionID); err != nil {
   613→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   614→	}
   615→
   616→	return nil
   617→}
   618→
   619→// handlePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   620→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   621→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   622→func handlePostTodo() error {
   623→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   624→	if err != nil {
   625→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   626→	}
   627→
   628→	// Get agent for logging context
   629→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   630→	if err != nil {
   631→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   632→	}
   633→
   634→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   635→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   636→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   637→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   638→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   639→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   640→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   641→	)
   642→
   643→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   644→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   645→	if !found {
   646→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   647→		return nil
   648→	}
   649→
   650→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   651→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   652→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   653→		return nil
   654→	}
   655→
   656→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   657→	modified, newFiles, deleted, err := DetectChangedFiles()
   658→	if err != nil {
   659→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   660→		return nil
   661→	}
   662→
   663→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   664→	if len(modified) == 0 && len(newFiles) == 0 && len(deleted) == 0 {
   665→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   666→		return nil
   667→	}
   668→
   669→	// Get git author
   670→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   671→	if err != nil {
   672→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   673→		return nil
   674→	}
   675→
   676→	// Get the active strategy
   677→	strat := GetStrategy()
   678→
   679→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   680→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   681→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   682→		return nil
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   686→	sessionID := paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   687→	if sessionID == "" {
   688→		sessionID = input.SessionID
   689→	}
   690→	entireSessionID := currentSessionIDWithFallback(sessionID)
   691→
   692→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   693→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(entireSessionID, taskToolUseID)
   694→
   695→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   696→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   697→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   698→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   699→	if todoContent == "" {
   700→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   701→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   702→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   703→		if todoCount > 0 {
   704→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   705→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   706→		}
   707→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   708→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   709→	}
   710→
   711→	// Get agent type from session state
   712→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   713→	if sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID); loadErr == nil && sessionState != nil {
   714→		agentType = sessionState.AgentType
   715→	}
   716→
   717→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   718→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   719→		SessionID:           entireSessionID,
   720→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   721→		ModifiedFiles:       modified,
   722→		NewFiles:            newFiles,
   723→		DeletedFiles:        deleted,
   724→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   725→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   726→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   727→		IsIncremental:       true,
   728→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   729→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   730→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   731→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   732→		AgentType:           agentType,
   733→	}
   734→
   735→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   736→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   737→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   738→		return nil
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   742→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   743→	return nil
   744→}
   745→
   746→// handlePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   747→func handlePreTask() error {
   748→	// Skip on default branch for strategies that don't allow it
   749→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranchForStrategy(); skip {
   750→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping on branch '%s' - create a feature branch to use Entire tracking\n", branchName)
   751→		return nil // Don't fail the hook, just skip
   752→	}
   753→
   754→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   755→	if err != nil {
   756→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   757→	}
   758→
   759→	// Get agent for logging context
   760→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   761→	if err != nil {
   762→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   763→	}
   764→
   765→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   766→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   767→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   768→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   769→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   770→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   771→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   772→	)
   773→
   774→	// Log context to stdout
   775→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   776→
   777→	// Capture pre-task state
   778→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   779→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	// Create "Starting agent" checkpoint
   783→	// This allows rewinding to the state just before the subagent began
   784→	if err := createStartingAgentCheckpoint(input); err != nil {
   785→		// Log warning but don't fail the hook - state was already captured
   786→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to create starting checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   787→	}
   788→
   789→	return nil
   790→}
   791→
   792→// createStartingAgentCheckpoint creates a checkpoint commit marking the start of a subagent.
   793→// This is called during PreToolUse[Task] hook.
   794→func createStartingAgentCheckpoint(input *TaskHookInput) error {
   795→	// Get git author
   796→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   797→	if err != nil {
   798→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   799→	}
   800→
   801→	// Get the active strategy
   802→	strat := GetStrategy()
   803→
   804→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   805→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   806→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure strategy setup: %w", err)
   807→	}
   808→
   809→	entireSessionID := currentSessionIDWithFallback(input.SessionID)
   810→
   811→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool_input for descriptive commit messages
   812→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   813→
   814→	// Get agent type from session state
   815→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   816→	if sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID); loadErr == nil && sessionState != nil {
   817→		agentType = sessionState.AgentType
   818→	}
   819→
   820→	// Build task checkpoint context for the "starting" checkpoint
   821→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   822→		SessionID:       entireSessionID,
   823→		ToolUseID:       input.ToolUseID,
   824→		TranscriptPath:  input.TranscriptPath,
   825→		AuthorName:      author.Name,
   826→		AuthorEmail:     author.Email,
   827→		SubagentType:    subagentType,
   828→		TaskDescription: taskDescription,
   829→		AgentType:       agentType,
   830→		// No file changes yet - this is the starting state
   831→		ModifiedFiles: nil,
   832→		NewFiles:      nil,
   833→		DeletedFiles:  nil,
   834→		// Mark as starting checkpoint (sequence 0)
   835→		IsIncremental:       true,
   836→		IncrementalSequence: 0,
   837→		IncrementalType:     strategy.IncrementalTypeTaskStart,
   838→	}
   839→
   840→	// Save the checkpoint
   841→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   842→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save starting checkpoint: %w", err)
   843→	}
   844→
   845→	shortID := input.ToolUseID
   846→	if len(shortID) > 12 {
   847→		shortID = shortID[:12]
   848→	}
   849→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created starting checkpoint for task %s\n", shortID)
   850→
   851→	return nil
   852→}
   853→
   854→// handlePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   855→func handlePostTask() error {
   856→	// Skip on default branch for strategies that don't allow it
   857→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranchForStrategy(); skip {
   858→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping on branch '%s' - create a feature branch to use Entire tracking\n", branchName)
   859→		return nil // Don't fail the hook, just skip
   860→	}
   861→
   862→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   863→	if err != nil {
   864→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   865→	}
   866→
   867→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   868→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   869→
   870→	// Get agent for logging context
   871→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   872→	if err != nil {
   873→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   874→	}
   875→
   876→	// Log parsed input context
   877→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   878→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   879→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   880→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   881→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   882→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   883→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   884→	)
   885→
   886→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   887→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   888→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   889→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   890→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   891→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   892→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   893→		}
   894→	}
   895→
   896→	// Log context to stdout
   897→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   898→
   899→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   900→	var modifiedFiles []string
   901→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   902→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   903→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   904→		if err != nil {
   905→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   906→		} else {
   907→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   908→		}
   909→	} else {
   910→		// Fall back to main transcript
   911→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   912→		if err != nil {
   913→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   914→		} else {
   915→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   916→		}
   917→	}
   918→
   919→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   920→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   921→	if err != nil {
   922→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   923→	}
   924→	newFiles, err := ComputeNewFilesFromTask(preState)
   925→	if err != nil {
   926→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute new files: %v\n", err)
   927→	}
   928→
   929→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   930→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   931→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   932→	if err != nil {
   933→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   934→	}
   935→
   936→	// Filter and normalize paths
   937→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   938→	relNewFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(newFiles, repoRoot)
   939→
   940→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   941→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   942→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   943→
   944→	// Get git author
   945→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   946→	if err != nil {
   947→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   948→	}
   949→
   950→	// Get the configured strategy
   951→	strat := GetStrategy()
   952→
   953→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place
   954→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   955→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   956→	}
   957→
   958→	entireSessionID := currentSessionIDWithFallback(input.SessionID)
   959→
   960→	// Get agent type from session state
   961→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   962→	if sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID); loadErr == nil && sessionState != nil {
   963→		agentType = sessionState.AgentType
   964→	}
   965→
   966→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   967→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handlePostTodo,
   968→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   969→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   970→		SessionID:              entireSessionID,
   971→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   972→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   973→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   974→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   975→		DeletedFiles:           nil, // TODO: compute deleted files
   976→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   977→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   978→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   979→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   980→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   981→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   982→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   983→		AgentType:              agentType,
   984→	}
   985→
   986→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   987→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   988→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   989→	}
   990→
   991→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   992→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   993→
   994→	return nil
   995→}
   996→
   997→// handleSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   998→// It reads session info from stdin and sets it as the current session.
   999→func handleSessionStart() error {
  1000→	// Get the agent for session ID transformation
  1001→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
  1002→	if err != nil {
  1003→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
  1004→	}
  1005→
  1006→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
  1007→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, os.Stdin)
  1008→	if err != nil {
  1009→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
  1010→	}
  1011→
  1012→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
  1013→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
  1014→		slog.String("hook", "session-start"),
  1015→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
  1016→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
  1017→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
  1018→	)
  1019→
  1020→	if input.SessionID == "" {
  1021→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
  1022→	}
  1023→
  1024→	// Generate the full Entire session ID (with date prefix) from the agent's session ID
  1025→	entireSessionID := paths.EntireSessionID(input.SessionID)
  1026→
  1027→	// Write session ID to current_session file
  1028→	if err := paths.WriteCurrentSession(entireSessionID); err != nil {
  1029→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set current session: %w", err)
  1030→	}
  1031→
  1032→	fmt.Printf("Current session set to: %s\n", entireSessionID)
  1033→	return nil
  1034→}
  1035→
  1036→// hookResponse represents a JSON response for Claude Code hooks.
  1037→// Used to control whether Claude continues processing the prompt.
  1038→type hookResponse struct {
  1039→	Continue   bool   `json:"continue"`
  1040→	StopReason string `json:"stopReason,omitempty"`
  1041→}
  1042→
  1043→// outputHookResponse outputs a JSON response to stdout for Claude Code hooks.
  1044→// When continueExec is false, Claude will block the current operation and show the reason to the user.
  1045→func outputHookResponse(continueExec bool, reason string) error {
  1046→	resp := hookResponse{
  1047→		Continue:   continueExec,
  1048→		StopReason: reason,
  1049→	}
  1050→	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
  1051→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
  1052→	}
  1053→	return nil
  1054→}
