    21→// SaveChanges saves a checkpoint to the shadow branch.
    22→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteTemporary for git operations.
    23→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error {
    24→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    25→	if err != nil {
    26→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    27→	}
    28→
    29→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
    30→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
    31→
    32→	// Load or initialize session state
    33→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→	// Initialize if state is nil OR BaseCommit is empty (can happen with partial state from warnings)
    38→	if state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
    39→		// Preserve existing AgentType if we have a partial state, otherwise use default
    40→		agentType := DefaultAgentType
    41→		if state != nil && state.AgentType != "" {
    42→			agentType = state.AgentType
    43→		}
    44→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, "") // No transcript path in fallback
    45→		if err != nil {
    46→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
    47→		}
    48→	}
    49→
    50→	// Get checkpoint store
    51→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// Check if shadow branch exists to report whether we created it
    57→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit)
    58→	branchExisted := store.ShadowBranchExists(state.BaseCommit)
    59→
    60→	// Use WriteTemporary to create the checkpoint
    61→	isFirstCheckpointOfSession := state.CheckpointCount == 0
    62→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
    63→		SessionID:         sessionID,
    64→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
    65→		ModifiedFiles:     ctx.ModifiedFiles,
    66→		NewFiles:          ctx.NewFiles,
    67→		DeletedFiles:      ctx.DeletedFiles,
    68→		MetadataDir:       ctx.MetadataDir,
    69→		MetadataDirAbs:    ctx.MetadataDirAbs,
    70→		CommitMessage:     ctx.CommitMessage,
    71→		AuthorName:        ctx.AuthorName,
    72→		AuthorEmail:       ctx.AuthorEmail,
    73→		IsFirstCheckpoint: isFirstCheckpointOfSession,
    74→	})
    75→	if err != nil {
    76→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write temporary checkpoint: %w", err)
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// If checkpoint was skipped due to deduplication (no changes), return early
    80→	if result.Skipped {
    81→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
    82→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
    83→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
    84→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
    85→			slog.Int("checkpoint_count", state.CheckpointCount),
    86→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
    87→		)
    88→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last checkpoint)\n")
    89→		return nil
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	// Update session state
    93→	state.CheckpointCount++
    94→
    95→	// Track touched files (modified, new, and deleted)
    96→	state.FilesTouched = mergeFilesTouched(state.FilesTouched, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
    97→
    98→	// On first checkpoint, store the initial transcript position
    99→	if isFirstCheckpointOfSession {
   100→		state.TranscriptLinesAtStart = ctx.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   101→		state.TranscriptUUIDAtStart = ctx.TranscriptUUIDAtStart
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	// Accumulate token usage
   105→	if ctx.TokenUsage != nil {
   106→		state.TokenUsage = accumulateTokenUsage(state.TokenUsage, ctx.TokenUsage)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	// Save updated state
   110→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	if !branchExisted {
   115→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created shadow branch '%s' and committed changes\n", shadowBranchName)
   116→	} else {
   117→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed changes to shadow branch '%s'\n", shadowBranchName)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Log checkpoint creation
   121→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   122→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   123→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   124→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   125→		slog.Int("checkpoint_count", state.CheckpointCount),
   126→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   127→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   128→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   129→		slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   130→		slog.Bool("branch_created", !branchExisted),
   131→	)
   132→
   133→	return nil
   134→}
   135→
   136→// SaveTaskCheckpoint saves a task checkpoint to the shadow branch.
   137→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteTemporaryTask for git operations.
   138→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx TaskCheckpointContext) error {
   139→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   140→	if err != nil {
   141→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	// Load session state
   145→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(ctx.SessionID)
   146→	if err != nil || state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
   147→		// Initialize if needed (including if BaseCommit is empty from partial warning state)
   148→		// Preserve existing AgentType if we have a partial state, otherwise use default
   149→		agentType := DefaultAgentType
   150→		if state != nil && state.AgentType != "" {
   151→			agentType = state.AgentType
   152→		}
   153→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, ctx.SessionID, agentType, "") // No transcript path in fallback
   154→		if err != nil {
   155→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session for task checkpoint: %w", err)
   156→		}
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	// Get checkpoint store
   160→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Check if shadow branch exists to report whether we created it
   166→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit)
   167→	branchExisted := store.ShadowBranchExists(state.BaseCommit)
   168→
   169→	// Compute metadata paths for commit message
   170→	sessionMetadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromEntireID(ctx.SessionID)
   171→	taskMetadataDir := TaskMetadataDir(sessionMetadataDir, ctx.ToolUseID)
   172→
   173→	// Generate commit message
   174→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   175→	if len(shortToolUseID) > 12 {
   176→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:12]
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	var messageSubject string
   180→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   181→		messageSubject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   182→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   183→			ctx.SubagentType,
   184→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   185→			ctx.TodoContent,
   186→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   187→			shortToolUseID,
   188→		)
   189→	} else {
   190→		messageSubject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   191→	}
   192→	commitMsg := trailers.FormatShadowTaskCommit(
   193→		messageSubject,
   194→		taskMetadataDir,
   195→		ctx.SessionID,
   196→	)
   197→
   198→	// Use WriteTemporaryTask to create the checkpoint
   199→	_, err = store.WriteTemporaryTask(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryTaskOptions{
   200→		SessionID:              ctx.SessionID,
   201→		BaseCommit:             state.BaseCommit,
   202→		ToolUseID:              ctx.ToolUseID,
   203→		AgentID:                ctx.AgentID,
   204→		ModifiedFiles:          ctx.ModifiedFiles,
   205→		NewFiles:               ctx.NewFiles,
   206→		DeletedFiles:           ctx.DeletedFiles,
   207→		TranscriptPath:         ctx.TranscriptPath,
   208→		SubagentTranscriptPath: ctx.SubagentTranscriptPath,
   209→		CheckpointUUID:         ctx.CheckpointUUID,
   210→		CommitMessage:          commitMsg,
   211→		AuthorName:             ctx.AuthorName,
   212→		AuthorEmail:            ctx.AuthorEmail,
   213→		IsIncremental:          ctx.IsIncremental,
   214→		IncrementalSequence:    ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   215→		IncrementalType:        ctx.IncrementalType,
   216→		IncrementalData:        ctx.IncrementalData,
   217→	})
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write task checkpoint: %w", err)
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	// Track touched files (modified, new, and deleted)
   223→	state.FilesTouched = mergeFilesTouched(state.FilesTouched, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   224→
   225→	// Save updated state
   226→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   227→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	if !branchExisted {
   231→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created shadow branch '%s' and committed task checkpoint\n", shadowBranchName)
   232→	} else {
   233→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task checkpoint to shadow branch '%s'\n", shadowBranchName)
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	// Log task checkpoint creation
   237→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   238→	attrs := []any{
   239→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   240→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "task"),
   241→		slog.String("checkpoint_uuid", ctx.CheckpointUUID),
   242→		slog.String("tool_use_id", ctx.ToolUseID),
   243→		slog.String("subagent_type", ctx.SubagentType),
   244→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   245→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   246→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   247→		slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   248→		slog.Bool("branch_created", !branchExisted),
   249→	}
   250→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   251→		attrs = append(attrs,
   252→			slog.Bool("is_incremental", true),
   253→			slog.String("incremental_type", ctx.IncrementalType),
   254→			slog.Int("incremental_sequence", ctx.IncrementalSequence),
   255→		)
   256→	}
   257→	logging.Info(logCtx, "task checkpoint saved", attrs...)
   258→
   259→	return nil
   260→}
   261→
   262→// mergeFilesTouched merges multiple file lists into existing touched files, deduplicating.
   263→func mergeFilesTouched(existing []string, fileLists ...[]string) []string {
   264→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   265→	for _, f := range existing {
   266→		seen[f] = true
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	for _, list := range fileLists {
   270→		for _, f := range list {
   271→			seen[f] = true
   272→		}
   273→	}
   274→
   275→	result := make([]string, 0, len(seen))
   276→	for f := range seen {
   277→		result = append(result, f)
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	// Sort for deterministic output
   281→	sort.Strings(result)
   282→	return result
   283→}
   284→
   285→// accumulateTokenUsage adds new token usage to existing accumulated usage.
   286→// If existing is nil, returns a copy of incoming. If incoming is nil, returns existing unchanged.
   287→func accumulateTokenUsage(existing, incoming *agent.TokenUsage) *agent.TokenUsage {
   288→	if incoming == nil {
   289→		return existing
   290→	}
   291→	if existing == nil {
   292→		// Return a copy to avoid sharing the pointer
   293→		return &agent.TokenUsage{
   294→			InputTokens:         incoming.InputTokens,
   295→			CacheCreationTokens: incoming.CacheCreationTokens,
   296→			CacheReadTokens:     incoming.CacheReadTokens,
   297→			OutputTokens:        incoming.OutputTokens,
   298→			APICallCount:        incoming.APICallCount,
   299→			SubagentTokens:      incoming.SubagentTokens,
   300→		}
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	// Accumulate values
   304→	existing.InputTokens += incoming.InputTokens
   305→	existing.CacheCreationTokens += incoming.CacheCreationTokens
   306→	existing.CacheReadTokens += incoming.CacheReadTokens
   307→	existing.OutputTokens += incoming.OutputTokens
   308→	existing.APICallCount += incoming.APICallCount
   309→
   310→	// Accumulate subagent tokens if present
   311→	if incoming.SubagentTokens != nil {
   312→		existing.SubagentTokens = accumulateTokenUsage(existing.SubagentTokens, incoming.SubagentTokens)
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	return existing
   316→}
   317→
   318→// deleteShadowBranch deletes a shadow branch by name using an existing repo handle.
   319→// Returns nil if the branch doesn't exist (idempotent).
   320→func deleteShadowBranch(repo *git.Repository, branchName string) error {
   321→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName)
   322→
   323→	// Check if reference exists
   324→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   325→	if err != nil {
   326→		// Branch doesn't exist - nothing to delete (idempotent)
   327→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Not an error condition - branch already gone
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	// Delete the reference
   331→	if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
   332→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete branch %s: %w", branchName, err)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	return nil
   336→}
   337→

   717→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
   718→// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
   719→// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
   720→//
   721→// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
   722→// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
   723→//
   724→// If there's an existing shadow branch with activity from a different worktree,
   725→// returns a ShadowBranchConflictError to allow the caller to inform the user.
   726→//
   727→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   728→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   729→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string) error {
   730→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   731→	if err != nil {
   732→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   733→	}
   734→
   735→	// Get current HEAD
   736→	head, err := repo.Head()
   737→	if err != nil {
   738→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	// Check if session already exists
   742→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   743→	if err != nil {
   744→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check session state: %w", err)
   745→	}
   746→
   747→	// Check for shadow branch conflict before proceeding
   748→	// This must happen even if session state exists but has no checkpoints yet
   749→	// (e.g., state was created by concurrent warning but conflict later resolved)
   750→	baseCommitHash := head.Hash().String()
   751→	if state == nil || state.CheckpointCount == 0 {
   752→		shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommitHash)
   753→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   754→
   755→		ref, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   756→		if refErr == nil {
   757→			// Shadow branch exists - check if it has commits from a different session
   758→			tipCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   759→			if commitErr == nil {
   760→				existingSessionID, found := trailers.ParseSession(tipCommit.Message)
   761→				if found && existingSessionID != sessionID {
   762→					// Check if the existing session has a state file
   763→					// existingSessionID is the full Entire session ID (YYYY-MM-DD-uuid) from the trailer
   764→					// We intentionally ignore load errors - treat them as "no state" (orphaned branch)
   765→					existingState, _ := s.loadSessionState(existingSessionID) //nolint:errcheck // error means no state
   766→					if existingState == nil {
   767→						// Orphaned shadow branch - no state file for the existing session
   768→						// Reset the branch so the new session can proceed
   769→						fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Resetting orphaned shadow branch '%s' (previous session %s has no state)\n",
   770→							shadowBranch, existingSessionID)
   771→						if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranch); err != nil {
   772→							return fmt.Errorf("failed to reset orphaned shadow branch: %w", err)
   773→						}
   774→					} else {
   775→						// Existing session has state - this is a real conflict
   776→						// (e.g., different worktree at same commit)
   777→						return &SessionIDConflictError{
   778→							ExistingSession: existingSessionID,
   779→							NewSession:      sessionID,
   780→							ShadowBranch:    shadowBranch,
   781→						}
   782→					}
   783→				}

   785→		}

   207→// WriteTemporaryTask writes a task checkpoint to a shadow branch.
   208→// Task checkpoints include both code changes and task-specific metadata.
   209→// Returns the commit hash of the created checkpoint.
   210→func (s *GitStore) WriteTemporaryTask(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryTaskOptions) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   211→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   212→
   213→	// Validate base commit - required for shadow branch naming
   214→	if opts.BaseCommit == "" {
   215→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, errors.New("BaseCommit is required for task checkpoint")
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	// Validate identifiers to prevent path traversal and malformed data
   219→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
   220→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("invalid task checkpoint options: %w", err)
   221→	}
   222→	if err := paths.ValidateToolUseID(opts.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   223→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("invalid task checkpoint options: %w", err)
   224→	}
   225→	if err := paths.ValidateAgentID(opts.AgentID); err != nil {
   226→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("invalid task checkpoint options: %w", err)
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	// Get shadow branch name
   230→	shadowBranchName := ShadowBranchNameForCommit(opts.BaseCommit)
   231→
   232→	// Get or create shadow branch
   233→	parentHash, baseTreeHash, err := s.getOrCreateShadowBranch(shadowBranchName)
   234→	if err != nil {
   235→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get shadow branch: %w", err)
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	// Collect all files to include in the commit
   239→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(opts.ModifiedFiles)+len(opts.NewFiles))
   240→	allFiles = append(allFiles, opts.ModifiedFiles...)
   241→	allFiles = append(allFiles, opts.NewFiles...)
   242→
   243→	// Build new tree with code changes (no metadata dir yet)
   244→	newTreeHash, err := s.buildTreeWithChanges(baseTreeHash, allFiles, opts.DeletedFiles, "", "")
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to build tree: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	// Add task metadata to tree
   250→	newTreeHash, err = s.addTaskMetadataToTree(newTreeHash, opts)
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to add task metadata: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Create the commit
   256→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, parentHash, opts.CommitMessage, opts.AuthorName, opts.AuthorEmail)
   257→	if err != nil {
   258→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to create commit: %w", err)
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	// Update shadow branch reference
   262→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   263→	ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
   264→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   265→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to update shadow branch reference: %w", err)
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	return commitHash, nil
   269→}

> AGENT

I found the bug. Here's the complete analysis:

## Root Cause

**Task checkpoints do NOT increment `CheckpointCount`**, but they DO create commits on the shadow branch.

Looking at the code:

1. **`SaveChanges`** (main session checkpoint) - `manual_commit_git.go:93`:
   ```go
   state.CheckpointCount++
   ```

2. **`SaveTaskCheckpoint`** (task/subagent checkpoint) - `manual_commit_git.go:222-228`:
   ```go
   // Track touched files (modified, new, and deleted)
   state.FilesTouched = mergeFilesTouched(...)
   // Save updated state
   if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil { ... }
   ```
   
   It updates `FilesTouched` but **never increments `CheckpointCount`**.

## The Bug Scenario

1. **Session A (`914e68cc`) starts**
   - `InitializeSession` creates state with `CheckpointCount: 0`

2. **User runs a Task (subagent) in Session A**
   - `PreToolUse[Task]` fires → `createStartingAgentCheckpoint` → `SaveTaskCheckpoint`
   - This creates a commit on the shadow branch (even with no file changes!)
   - `CheckpointCount` stays at 0

3. **User closes Claude without main agent making file changes**
   - `Stop` hook fires but `commitWithMetadata` returns early ("No files were modified")
   - Session A state: `CheckpointCount: 0`, shadow branch has commits

4. **Session B (`f9b37e51`) tries to start**
   - `InitializeSession` checks: `state == nil || state.CheckpointCount == 0` → TRUE
   - Sees shadow branch with commits tagged `914e68cc`
   - Loads Session A's state file (exists with `CheckpointCount: 0`)
   - Returns `SessionIDConflictError`

5. **User sees the error message about "orphaning existing work"**
   - But working tree is clean (task changes were undone, committed, or never existed)

## The `"Agent"` Type Mystery

The `"Agent"` fallback type could happen if:
- The session was created before `AgentType` was added (backward compat)
- OR `SaveChanges`/`SaveTaskCheckpoint` was called before `InitializeSession`

Looking at `SaveTaskCheckpoint` lines 148-157:
```go
if state != nil && state.AgentType != "" {
    agentType = state.AgentType
}
state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, ctx.SessionID, agentType, "")
```

If the state exists but has empty `AgentType`, it falls back to `DefaultAgentType = "Agent"`.

## Recommended Fix

**Option 1**: Add a separate counter for task checkpoints:
```go
// In SaveTaskCheckpoint:
state.TaskCheckpointCount++
```

Then modify conflict detection to check both:
```go
if state == nil || (state.CheckpointCount == 0 && state.TaskCheckpointCount == 0) {
```

**Option 2**: Have `SaveTaskCheckpoint` also increment `CheckpointCount`:
```go
// In SaveTaskCheckpoint:
state.CheckpointCount++  // Or a flag like HasCheckpoints bool
```

**Option 3**: Change conflict detection to actually check shadow branch for commits, not rely on counter:
```go
// Count commits on shadow branch instead of trusting CheckpointCount
```

Would you like me to implement one of these fixes?

> DEVELOPER

but if the task didn't do any file changes, we shouldn't do a shadow branch commit, or?

> AGENT

You're absolutely right. Looking at `createStartingAgentCheckpoint`:

```go
ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
    // ...
    // No file changes yet - this is the starting state
    ModifiedFiles: nil,
    NewFiles:      nil,
    DeletedFiles:  nil,
    // ...
}

if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil { ... }
```

It creates a commit on the shadow branch with **no file changes** - just metadata. This is the actual root cause.

The "Starting agent" checkpoint was intended to allow rewinding to before the task started. But if we're creating shadow branch commits without file changes:

1. It creates/updates the shadow branch unnecessarily
2. Tags the commit with the session ID
3. Causes the conflict detection to trigger even when there's no actual work to orphan

**The fix should be**: Skip creating shadow branch commits when there are no file changes.

Looking at `handlePostTodo` (incremental checkpoints), it already does this:
```go
if len(modified) == 0 && len(newFiles) == 0 && len(deleted) == 0 {
    fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
    return nil
}
```

But `createStartingAgentCheckpoint` and `handlePostTask` don't have this check.

**The simplest fix**: Add the same early-return check to both:
1. `createStartingAgentCheckpoint` - skip if no changes (which is always the case for "starting" checkpoint)
2. Or better: remove the "starting" checkpoint commit entirely, just capture pre-task state locally

Want me to implement this fix? The starting checkpoint's value was metadata tracking, but we can capture that locally without creating a shadow branch commit.

   747→func handlePreTask() error {

   783→	// This allows rewinding to the state just before the subagent began

   792→// createStartingAgentCheckpoint creates a checkpoint commit marking the start of a subagent.
   793→// This is called during PreToolUse[Task] hook.
   794→func createStartingAgentCheckpoint(input *TaskHookInput) error {
   795→	// Get git author
   796→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   797→	if err != nil {
   798→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   799→	}
   800→
   801→	// Get the active strategy
   802→	strat := GetStrategy()
   803→
   804→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   805→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   806→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure strategy setup: %w", err)
   807→	}
   808→
   809→	entireSessionID := currentSessionIDWithFallback(input.SessionID)
   810→
   811→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool_input for descriptive commit messages
   812→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   813→
   814→	// Get agent type from session state
   815→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   816→	if sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID); loadErr == nil && sessionState != nil {
   817→		agentType = sessionState.AgentType
   818→	}
   819→
   820→	// Build task checkpoint context for the "starting" checkpoint
   821→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   822→		SessionID:       entireSessionID,
   823→		ToolUseID:       input.ToolUseID,
   824→		TranscriptPath:  input.TranscriptPath,
   825→		AuthorName:      author.Name,
   826→		AuthorEmail:     author.Email,
   827→		SubagentType:    subagentType,
   828→		TaskDescription: taskDescription,
   829→		AgentType:       agentType,
   830→		// No file changes yet - this is the starting state
   831→		ModifiedFiles: nil,
   832→		NewFiles:      nil,
   833→		DeletedFiles:  nil,
   834→		// Mark as starting checkpoint (sequence 0)
   835→		IsIncremental:       true,
   836→		IncrementalSequence: 0,
   837→		IncrementalType:     strategy.IncrementalTypeTaskStart,
   838→	}
   839→
   840→	// Save the checkpoint
   841→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   842→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save starting checkpoint: %w", err)
   843→	}
   844→
   845→	shortID := input.ToolUseID
   846→	if len(shortID) > 12 {
   847→		shortID = shortID[:12]
   848→	}
   849→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created starting checkpoint for task %s\n", shortID)
   850→
   851→	return nil

> AGENT

Done. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Changes Made

**File: `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`**

1. **Removed the call to `createStartingAgentCheckpoint`** in `handlePreTask` (lines 782-787)
   - The pre-task state capture (`CapturePreTaskState`) is still performed locally
   - Added a comment explaining why we don't create a shadow branch commit here

2. **Removed the `createStartingAgentCheckpoint` function** entirely (was lines 792-852)
   - This function created empty commits on the shadow branch with no file changes
   - These commits were causing the bug where sessions with only task activity had `CheckpointCount: 0` but commits on the shadow branch

## How This Fixes the Bug

Before:
1. Task starts → `createStartingAgentCheckpoint` → commit on shadow branch (no files)
2. Task completes without file changes → no additional commits
3. Session state: `CheckpointCount: 0`, shadow branch has commits
4. New session: `SessionIDConflictError` even though working tree is clean

After:
1. Task starts → only local state captured (no shadow branch commit)
2. Task completes without file changes → no commits created
3. Session state: `CheckpointCount: 0`, **no shadow branch**
4. New session: starts cleanly

Shadow branch commits are now only created when there are **actual file changes**, matching user expectations.

> DEVELOPER

The comment on line 779 states "commits are only created when the task actually makes file changes (in handlePostTask/handlePostTodo)". However, this is not entirely accurate. Looking at handlePostTask (lines 899-917), it creates a TaskCheckpointContext with IsIncremental not set (defaults to false). For the auto-commit strategy, this means the checkpoint logic in commitTaskCodeToActive will skip creating a commit if there are no file changes (line 588 returns early when !hasFileChanges && !isTaskStart).

This means if a task completes with no file changes, no checkpoint commit is created at all (as intended by this fix). Consider clarifying the comment to be more precise: "We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during task completion (handlePostTask/handlePostTodo) only if the task resulted in file changes."

This change removes the only production code path that ever sets IncrementalType to IncrementalTypeTaskStart. The constant, related logic, and tests still exist in the codebase but are now unreachable:

IncrementalTypeTaskStart constant in strategy/strategy.go:30
TaskStart-specific logic in auto_commit.go:584 and 636-644 (empty commit creation)
TaskStart-specific formatting in messages.go:82-84
Test TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveTaskCheckpoint_TaskStartCreatesEmptyCommit that tests this behavior
After this PR, no code sets ctx.IncrementalType = IncrementalTypeTaskStart. The handlePostTodo handler sets it to input.ToolName (e.g., "TodoWrite"), and handlePostTask doesn't set IsIncremental at all.

While removing the starting checkpoint is the correct fix for the bug, consider also removing the now-dead TaskStart code paths and tests in a follow-up change to avoid confusion and maintenance burden.     24→// FormatSubagentStartMessage formats a commit message for when a subagent starts.
    25→// Format: "Starting '<agent-type>' agent: <description> (<tool-use-id>)"
    26→//
    27→// Edge cases:
    28→//   - Empty description: "Starting '<agent-type>' agent (<tool-use-id>)"
    29→//   - Empty agentType: "Starting agent: <description> (<tool-use-id>)"
    30→//   - Both empty: "Task: <tool-use-id>"
    31→func FormatSubagentStartMessage(agentType, description, toolUseID string) string {
    32→	return formatSubagentMessage("Starting", agentType, description, toolUseID)
    33→}
    34→
    35→// FormatSubagentEndMessage formats a commit message for when a subagent completes.
    36→// Format: "Completed '<agent-type>' agent: <description> (<tool-use-id>)"
    37→//
    38→// Edge cases:
    39→//   - Empty description: "Completed '<agent-type>' agent (<tool-use-id>)"
    40→//   - Empty agentType: "Completed agent: <description> (<tool-use-id>)"
    41→//   - Both empty: "Task: <tool-use-id>"
    42→func FormatSubagentEndMessage(agentType, description, toolUseID string) string {
    43→	return formatSubagentMessage("Completed", agentType, description, toolUseID)
    44→}
    45→
    46→// formatSubagentMessage is a shared helper for start/end messages.
    47→func formatSubagentMessage(verb, agentType, description, toolUseID string) string {
    48→	// Both empty - fall back to simple format
    49→	if agentType == "" && description == "" {
    50→		return "Task: " + toolUseID
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	// Truncate description if needed
    54→	if description != "" {
    55→		description = TruncateDescription(description, MaxDescriptionLength)
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Build message based on what fields are present
    59→	if agentType != "" && description != "" {
    60→		return fmt.Sprintf("%s '%s' agent: %s (%s)", verb, agentType, description, toolUseID)
    61→	}
    62→	if agentType != "" {
    63→		return fmt.Sprintf("%s '%s' agent (%s)", verb, agentType, toolUseID)
    64→	}
    65→	// agentType is empty, description is present
    66→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s agent: %s (%s)", verb, description, toolUseID)
    67→}
    68→

> AGENT

All tests pass and lint is clean. Here's a summary of the cleanup:

## Summary of Changes

### Bug Fix (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`)
- Removed `createStartingAgentCheckpoint()` function and its call from `handlePreTask`
- Pre-task state is still captured locally via `CapturePreTaskState()` for computing new files

### Dead Code Cleanup

**`strategy/strategy.go`**
- Removed `IncrementalTypeTaskStart` constant

**`strategy/auto_commit.go`**
- Simplified `commitTaskCodeToActive()` to skip commits when there are no file changes (removed TaskStart special case that created empty marker commits)

**`strategy/messages.go`**
- Removed `FormatSubagentStartMessage()` function
- Simplified `FormatIncrementalSubject()` to always delegate to `FormatIncrementalMessage()` (kept parameters for API compatibility)

**`strategy/auto_commit_test.go`**
- Removed `TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveTaskCheckpoint_TaskStartCreatesEmptyCommit`
- Renamed and simplified remaining test to `TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveTaskCheckpoint_NoChangesSkipsCommit`

**`strategy/messages_test.go`**
- Removed `TestFormatSubagentStartMessage`
- Removed TaskStart test cases from `TestFormatIncrementalSubject`

> DEVELOPER

I also got this feedback: 

Consider adding an integration test that specifically reproduces the bug scenario described in the PR:

Session A starts (InitializeSession creates state with CheckpointCount: 0)
A Task is started in Session A (PreToolUse[Task] fires)
Session A closes without the task making file changes (Stop hook fires, no checkpoint created)
Session B tries to start (should succeed now)
This would ensure the bug doesn't regress. The test would verify that Session B can start successfully when Session A has state with CheckpointCount == 0 but no shadow branch commits (or only commits that get properly detected as orphaned).

The existing test TestSessionIDConflict_OrphanedBranchIsReset is close but doesn't test the exact scenario where a task starts but makes no changes.


But I also wonder: The bug was code that was doing to much, so having a test that checks a checkpoint isn't made feels a bit strange.

> AGENT

You raise a good point. The bug was caused by code doing too much (creating commits when it shouldn't). Now that code is gone.

An integration test for this would essentially be:
1. Call `handlePreTask`
2. Assert no shadow branch commit was created
3. Assert Session B can start

But this is testing for the *absence* of behavior - verifying code doesn't do something. That's a bit awkward because:

1. **If someone re-adds the "starting checkpoint" feature**, they'd likely update or remove the test too
2. **The fix is structural** - the code path that created the problematic commits simply doesn't exist anymore
3. **Existing tests already cover the relevant logic** - `TestSessionIDConflict_OrphanedBranchIsReset` tests orphan detection, and the unit tests for `handlePreTask` would show if someone adds checkpoint creation back

The comment in `handlePreTask` documents *why* we don't create commits here:

```go
// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
// task completion (handlePostTask/handlePostTodo) only if the task resulted
// in file changes.
```

If someone wants to re-add starting checkpoints in the future, they'd need to:
1. Add the code back
2. Read this comment explaining why it was removed
3. Solve the underlying problem (task checkpoints not incrementing `CheckpointCount`)

I'd skip the integration test unless you feel strongly about it.
